[UU] We usually translate “nation” as “nasud” so change “inged” here to “nasud”. Please check again “nations that are at ease” (so ASV, NRSV). The Hebrew word in question is ¤Ãná'aH (sha’anan) which has the idea of a careless, even arrogant attitude. NAB “the complacent nations.” Here it suggests that the nations take for granted that God will never punish them just because he hasn't already done so. Thus they presume on the grace and patience of the Lord. The IBS translate it as “nasod nga nagpahayahay lang” and has a footnote which says “nagapahayahay lang samtang gilisod-lisod nila ang ubang mga nasod labi na ang siyudad sa Jerusalem.”… The meaning of “a little they furthered the disaster” in RSV is this: In the days before the exile, the LORD was angry with his own people because of their idolatry. He planned to punish them moderately, and to use heathen nations to do so. However, these heathen nations (primarily the Assyrians and Babylonians) grew proud of their own strength and attacked the LORD's people with a ferocity that was beyond measure.
